### greet-zh

```

```

```

```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCP は届いたことと順序を保証しますが、UDP は保証せず、そのぶん速く軽いです。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para actualizar o limpiar tu rama local antes de compartirla, porque deja un historial lineal. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es compartida o pública, porque no reescribe commits que otros pueden tener.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、面向连接的：先握手建立连接，数据有确认、丢了会重传、按顺序交付；UDP 则是无连接、不保证送达和顺序，但开销小、延迟低。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when your branch is still yours alone and you want a clean, linear history before sharing it. Merge when the branch is already shared or public, since rewriting pushed history breaks everyone else's copy.
```

```

  [partner message] 在 `bun test` 后面直接跟文件路径，比如：

```
bun test tests/foo.test.ts
```

也可以只写文件名的一部分，bun 会按路径里包含的字符串匹配。要只跑某个用例，加 `-t "用例名"`。
```

```
  function load(v) {
```

```
  buildCats();
```

```
<style>
  .k-app { padding: 16px; max-width: 360px; margin: 0 auto; background: var(--d-bg); color: var(--d-ink); font-family: inherit; }
  .k-card { background: var(--d-card); border: 1px solid var(--d-line); border-radius: 14px; padding: 16px; }
  .k-sum-label { font-size: 13px; color: var(--d-muted); }
  .k-sum-value { font-size: 36px; font-weight: 600; color: var(--d-accent); margin: 4px 0;  … Content = '记下';

  }

  function startEdit(f) {
    editingId = f.id;
    $('t').value = f.time;
    $('ml').value = f.ml;
    $('addtitle').textContent = '修改这一笔';
    $('save').textContent = '保存修改';

    $('addbox').open = true;

  function save() {

    if (!/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(time) || !(ml > 0)) return;
    if (editingId !== null) {
      const f = feeds.find(x => x.id === editingId);
      if (f) { f.time = time; f.ml = ml; }
    } else {
      feeds.push({ id: Date.now(), day: todayStr(), time: time, ml: ml });
    }
    ducter.set('feeds', feeds);
    resetForm();
    $('addbox').open = false;
    render();
  }

  function removeCurrent() {
    feeds = feeds.filter(x => x.id !== editingId);
    ducter.set('feeds', feeds);
    resetForm();
    $('addbox').open = false;
    render();
  }

  $('save').addEventListener('click', save);
  $('del').addEventListener('click', removeCurrent);

  function load() {
    feeds = ducter.get('feeds') || [];
    render();
  }

  ducter.onChange(load);
  resetForm();
  load();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"id":1,"day":"2026-11-28","time":"06:10","ml":120},{"id":2,"day":"2026-11-28","time":"09:05","ml":90}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

```
    <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="open">4</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">Tick each one off as it lands</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">Waiting on someone</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <ul class="k-list" id="list"></ul>
  </div>
</main>
<style>
  .k-row { width:100%; text-align:left; background:none; border:0; color:var(--d-ink); display:flex; gap:12px; align-items:flex-start; padding:12px 0; cursor:pointer; font:inherit; }
  .k-row[aria-pressed="true"] .k-row-main { text-decoration:line-through; color:var(--d-muted); }
  .k-box { flex:0 0 22px; height:22px; border:2px solid var(--d-line); border-radius:6px; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; margin-top:2px; font-size:14px; line-height:1; }
  .k-row[aria-pressed="true"] .k-box { background:var(--d-good); border-color:var(--d-good); color:var(--d-bg); }
  .k-row-main { display:block; font-weight:600; }
  .k-row-sub { display:block; color:var(--d-muted); font-size:13px; margin-top:2px; }
</style>
<script>
  const PRS = ducter.get("prs") || [];
  let landed = {};

  function render() {
    const list = documen … n.innerHTML =
        '<span class="k-box">' + (landed[pr.id] ? '✓' : '') + '</span>' +
        '<span><span class="k-row-main"></span><span class="k-row-sub"></span></span>';
      btn.querySelector('.k-row-main').textContent = pr.repo + ' · ' + pr.title;
      btn.querySelector('.k-row-sub').textContent = pr.waiting;
      btn.addEventListener('click', () => toggle(pr.id));
      li.appendChild(btn);
      list.appendChild(li);
    });
    const open = PRS.filter(p => !landed[p.id]).length;
    document.getElementById('open').textContent = open;
    document.getElementById('sub').textContent = open === 0
      ? 'All landed'
      : 'Tick each one off as it lands';
    ducter.summary(open + ' waiting, ' + (PRS.length - open) + ' landed');
  }

  function toggle(id) {
    landed[id] = !landed[id];
    if (!landed[id]) delete landed[id];
    ducter.set('landed', landed);
    render();
  }

  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('landed')).then(v => {
    landed = (v && typeof v === 'object') ? v : {};
    render();
  });
  ducter.onChange(() => {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('landed')).then(v => {
      landed = (v && typeof v === 'object') ? v : {};
      render();
    });
  });
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"ducter-412","repo":"wey-gu/ducter#412","title":"Compaction cut","waiting":"Waiting on Weizhen's review, since Monday"},{"id":"mem-10287","repo":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","title":"Retry on 502","waiting":"On CI, failing"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","repo":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","title":"Flaky compaction test","waiting":"Waiting on Alice, since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter-418","repo":"wey-gu/ducter#418","title":"Apps bar","waiting":"Draft, not yet ready for review"}]}
```

```
<div class="k-sum"><div class="k-sum-label">出差花销合计</div><div class="k-sum-value k-none" id="total">还没记</div><div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">记下第一笔，合计就出来</div></div>
<h2 class="k-h">花销明细</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div><div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>还没记。记下一笔，这里就会列出来。</div></div>
<details class="k-add"><summary>记一笔</summary>
<div class="k-field"><label>出差名称</label><input class="k-input" id="trip"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="如 11/20"></div>
<div class="k-chips"><button class="k-chip" data-cat="交通" aria-pressed="true">交通</button><button class="k-chip" data-cat="住宿" aria-pressed="false">住宿</button></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>金额</label><input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal"></div>
<button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button>
</details>
</main>
<script>
let cat="交通";
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
document.querySelectorAll("[data-cat]").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{cat=b.dataset.cat;document.querySelectorAll("[data-cat]").forEach(x=>x.setAttribute("aria-pressed",x===b?"true":"false"))});
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  const list=$("list");list.innerHTML="";
  xs.forEach(x=>{
    const r=document.createElement("div");r.className="k-row";
    const m=document.createElement("div");m.className="k-row-main";m.textContent=x.trip;
    const s=document.createElement("div");s.className="k-row-sub";s.textContent=(x.day||"")+" "+(x.cat||"");
    const v=document.createElement("div");v.className="k-row-value";v.textContent="¥"+Number(x.amt||0).toLocaleString("en-US");
    r.append(m,s,v);list.append(r);
  });
  const total=xs.reduce((a,x)=>a+Number(x.amt||0),0);
  $("empty").hidden=xs.length>0;
  $("total").textContent=xs.length?"¥"+total.toLocaleString("en-US"):"还没记";
  $("total").className=xs.length?"k-sum-value":"k-sum-value k-none";
  $("sub").textContent=xs.length?xs.length+" 笔":"记下第一笔，合计就出来";
  ducter.summary(xs.length?xs.length+" 笔,合计 ¥"+total.toLocaleString("en-US"):"还没记");
}
$("save").onclick=()=>{
  const amt=$("amt").value;if(!amt)return;
  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  xs.push({trip:$("trip").value||"出差",day:$("day").value,cat:cat,amt:Number(amt)});
  ducter.set("items",xs);
  $("amt").value="";draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21 · 高铁 G1 上海虹桥→北京南 07:00","day":"11/20","cat":"交通","amt":662},{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21 · 亚朵酒店（望京）两晚","day":"11/20","cat":"住宿","amt":1036}]}
```


### wp-pr-babysitter-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 帮我启动一个 codex 的 loop agent，名字就叫 Nowledge Mem PR babysitter（之类的名字），在 Nowledge Bridge 里你起好名字，我可以让我自己的 agent 在 PR 到一定阶段之后把它们丢给你，然后你就可以帮忙盯着，做需要的修改修复 ci failure，或者如果涉及到别的人的 review，帮忙做修复，如果你能自决修复的，就不断修复 push 然后看着，直到工作可以被合并，再 bridge 回信表示工作做完了

我们 bridge 不知道是否有唤醒能力，如果没有，暂时需要你自己去 loop 唤醒自己读取 bridge 信箱
  [partner message] 好，我来照看：Nowledge Mem PR babysitter 已经在 Nowledge Bridge 里挂好了，你的其他 agent 可以在那里找到它并把 PR 交过来。它会盯 CI 和 review，能自己修的就修、推送、继续盯，直到可以合并，再通过 Bridge 回信告诉对方。

它不会自己合并，也不会在别人的 review 线程里发言，这些都要你点头。另外我没法把它固定到 codex 上，它会按每件活的需要选 agent。Bridge 的唤醒我这边没法确认，但它设计成有活来了才叫醒它，不用我自己轮询信箱。
  [also used tools] keep_make, keep_change
--- what Ducter sent through Bridge (other agents see this, the user doesn't):
```
